## `AFG-2821-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Emissions-PEMS Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Tractor PTO Shaft Torque-Speed & Three-Point Hitch Lift Capacity

## `AGO-2821-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Powershift-Tractor Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for High-Horsepower Tractor Continuously Variable Transmission Assembler
- **Intro:** Powershift-Tractor Operator — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for High-Horsepower Tractor Continuously Variable Transmission Assembler — Verify Cold State and Apply Lockout/Tagout (LOTO): Engine oil temperature < 30°C; battery disconnect switch in OFF position; parking brake engaged with 100% lever travel.
